</div></div><div class="group/line flex border-l-[3px] border-l-transparent" data-diff-type="normal" data-diff-line="25" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><span class="flex items-start justify-end select-none pr-2 font-mono text-xs pl-1 text-text-400 border-r border-border-400" data-line-number="25" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem; width: 2.3rem;"> 25</span><span class="flex items-start justify-center font-mono text-xs select-none relative w-3 text-text-500" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem;"><span> </span></span><div class="flex-1 flex items-start pl-0 pr-2 group-data-[scrollable]/overlay:pr-6 min-w-0 font-mono" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><code class="font-mono text-xs break-all" style="line-height: 1.375rem; white-space: pre-wrap;">  - Binary Search       : pencarian berdasarkan ID (O(log n))</code>
</div></div><div class="group/line flex border-l-[3px] border-l-transparent" data-diff-type="normal" data-diff-line="26" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><span class="flex items-start justify-end select-none pr-2 font-mono text-xs pl-1 text-text-400 border-r border-border-400" data-line-number="26" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem; width: 2.3rem;"> 26</span><span class="flex items-start justify-center font-mono text-xs select-none relative w-3 text-text-500" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem;"><span> </span></span><div class="flex-1 flex items-start pl-0 pr-2 group-data-[scrollable]/overlay:pr-6 min-w-0 font-mono" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><code class="font-mono text-xs break-all" style="line-height: 1.375rem; white-space: pre-wrap;">  - Bubble Sort         : pengurutan berdasarkan ID ascending (O(n^2))</code>
</div></div><div class="group/line flex border-l-[3px] border-l-transparent" data-diff-type="normal" data-diff-line="27" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><span class="flex items-start justify-end select-none pr-2 font-mono text-xs pl-1 text-text-400 border-r border-border-400" data-line-number="27" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem; width: 2.3rem;"> 27</span><span class="flex items-start justify-center font-mono text-xs select-none relative w-3 text-text-500" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem;"><span> </span></span><div class="flex-1 flex items-start pl-0 pr-2 group-data-[scrollable]/overlay:pr-6 min-w-0 font-mono" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><code class="font-mono text-xs break-all" style="line-height: 1.375rem; white-space: pre-wrap;">  - Selection Sort      : pengurutan berdasarkan nama A-Z (O(n^2))</code>
</div></div><div class="group/line flex border-l-[3px] border-l-transparent" data-diff-type="normal" data-diff-line="28" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><span class="flex items-start justify-end select-none pr-2 font-mono text-xs pl-1 text-text-400 border-r border-border-400" data-line-number="28" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem; width: 2.3rem;"> 28</span><span class="flex items-start justify-center font-mono text-xs select-none relative w-3 text-text-500" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem;"><span> </span></span><div class="flex-1 flex items-start pl-0 pr-2 group-data-[scrollable]/overlay:pr-6 min-w-0 font-mono" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><code class="font-mono text-xs break-all" style="line-height: 1.375rem; white-space: pre-wrap;">  - Insertion Sort      : pengurutan berdasarkan jumlah tamu desc (O(n^2))</code>
</div></div><div class="group/line flex border-l-[3px] border-l-transparent" data-diff-type="normal" data-diff-line="29" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><span class="flex items-start justify-end select-none pr-2 font-mono text-xs pl-1 text-text-400 border-r border-border-400" data-line-number="29" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem; width: 2.3rem;"> 29</span><span class="flex items-start justify-center font-mono text-xs select-none relative w-3 text-text-500" data-prose-review-ignore="" style="min-height: 1.375rem; line-height: 1.375rem;"><span> </span></span><div class="flex-1 flex items-start pl-0 pr-2 group-data-[scrollable]/overlay:pr-6 min-w-0 font-mono" style="min-height: 1.375rem;"><code class="font-mono text-xs break-all" style="line-height: 1.375rem; white-space: pre-wrap;"><span data-prose-review-ignore="">&nbsp;</span></code>
